  summary: Merged and accepted `/my_codex/owner_dev_3` bugfix commit `d6bf7f51e635433e85352d1394a93c1f8d76b218` (`Validate followup image ref text misuse`). `followup_task` and `followup_external_task` now fail fast when explicit image placeholder text such as `[image:image-1]`, `[image: image-1]`, `<image attachment_id=image-1>`, or local image open tags are written into plain `message` or text content parts. - commit: e18cf9b84a

## Known Issues
- Workflow smoke run `wf_1785737385_1` exposed event routing/status bugs before it was aborted: `workflow_status` stayed at `runner_starting` while workflow agents had spawned, and the run consumed an unrelated `/my_codex/owner_dev` DMG completion/reviewer payload as workflow-internal review/fix input. The workflow run and its child agents were aborted/closed; dev-3 stayed clean. This is separate from the right-panel visualization feature but should inform workflow progress UI validation.
- 2026-07-27 用户反馈 root-worker 客户端运行时间长会变卡且偶现黑屏，并询问 compact 后客户端是否仍保存旧 items。只读确认：compact row 会把 compact 前 entries 从主可见 cell 列表折叠进 `archivedCells`，且展开详情按需加载/折叠丢弃；但客户端主 `Thread.turns` state 仍保留 compact 前原始 turns/items，`buildConversationState` / `buildCommandLookup` / context usage / thread analysis 等仍会遍历全量 `thread.turns`。当前也未看到 React error boundary 或全局 renderer error 捕获。后续应作为独立 root-worker 性能/稳定性 bugfix：compact 后对客户端 state 引入可恢复的 item pruning/archival boundary 或后端 read snapshot 裁剪策略，并补 error boundary/logging 与长线程回归。
- 2026-07-31 用户再次观察到 `/my_codex/owner_dev_3` thread compact 后没有显示 init context item。此前 2026-07-24 已有同类反馈。需要后续独立确认这是 UI 展示缺失、compact replacement history 未保留/未注入、还是 runtime 初始化上下文恢复链路问题；当前排查 external project thread no-response 时必须避免把 compact/init-context display 缺失误判为 provider input/context 未注入。
- 2026-07-31 用户再次指出模型在收到完整 command exit 事件后经常说“只收到了事件，没有收到具体内容”。当前怀疑是 `poll_event` tool result 只把 `sourceHint=command_exit` 暴露给模型，但对应的具体 `CommandExecutionNotification`（command id、exit code、output）没有在同一次/下一次 model-visible input 中正确注入，或没有和 `poll_event` 唤醒建立可用关联。后续应作为独立 runtime bugfix 排查 pending input selection、command notification payload、request construction 注入和 command_id/output 关联，而不是简单归因于 PM 误用。
- 2026-07-23 after `Move live removal to lifecycle runtime` was amended from `897a7e7045` to `d7e4e22ea6`, all three fixed dev checkouts stayed clean but pointed at the amended-out old commit. Because this is not a fast-forward, PM did not reset them automatically; before assigning new work to any dev checkout, explicitly realign the clean branch to main `d7e4e22ea6` or record why it remains pending.
- 2026-07-23 `rtk cargo test -p app-server --test all thread_start_preserves_client_supplied_root_agent_path --quiet` 仍被既有 app-server suite 编译错误阻塞，未执行到目标 Project path 回归测试。当前错误包括 `Turn.lifecycle_status` 字段已不存在、`McpServerStatusUpdatedNotification.lifecycle_status` 字段已不存在、`ThreadUnsubscribeResponse.lifecycle_status` 字段已改为 `status`，以及 `ThreadLifecycleStatus::Initializing` 未在旧 match 中覆盖。生产 `rtk cargo build -p app-server --bin app-server` 通过。
- 2026-07-22 PM 验收时多次观察到 `poll_event` 对后台 `exec_command` 只返回 `sourceHint: command_exit`，但没有把命令 stdout/stderr/exit summary 注入给模型；实际命令已结束，需要同步复跑才拿到结果。这与用户反馈“经常出现命令已经完成但是模型似乎没收到”一致。只读确认：`poll_event` tool result 按设计只携带 source hint；命令输出应由 `ResponseItem::CommandExecutionNotification { kind: Exit, output }` 作为 pending input 在下一次 request construction 注入。当前已知应排查 `notify_on=exit` full transcript / `notify_on=output` residual output 选择、`output=None` 的空通知、以及 pending input 被 wake 后是否真正进入下一轮 model-visible history。
- 原 `.codex/pm-progress.md` 中记录的 thread 重构上下文已过期，且引用的 `spec/` 文档已删除；后续若继续推进该主题，需要重新建立可执行拆分计划。
